m_dry - payload = (m_dry + payload) * (mass_ratio - 1)\nbase_fuel_mass = (dry_mass + payload) * (mass_ratio - 1)\nprint(f'Base fuel mass (Tsiolkovsky): {base_fuel_mass:.0f} kg')\n\n# Step 2: Propellant boil-off losses (LOX/LH2 has higher boil-off rate)\n# Typical boil-off rates: LOX ~0.1%/day, LH2 ~0.3%/day, combined ~0.2%/day for LOX/LH2\nboil_off_rate_per_day = 0.002  # 0.2% per day for LOX/LH2\nboil_off_loss = base_fuel_mass * boil_off_rate_per_day * transfer_time\nprint(f'Boil-off loss ({boil_off_rate_per_day*100:.1f}%/day over {transfer_time:.1f} days): {boil_off_loss:.0f} kg')\n\n# Step 3: Settling losses at ignition (ullage and residuals)\nsettling_loss_pct = 0.02  # 2% typical for liquid propellants\nsettling_loss = base_fuel_mass * settling_loss_pct\nprint(f'Settling losses ({settling_loss_pct*100:.0f}%): {settling_loss:.0f} kg')\n\n# Step 4: Operational reserve (mission safety margin)\nreserve_pct = 0.05  # 5% reserve\nfuel_before_reserve = base_fuel_mass + boil_off_loss + settling_loss\nfuel_reserve = fuel_before_reserve * reserve_pct\nprint(f'Fuel reserve ({reserve_pct*100:.0f}%): {fuel_reserve:.0f} kg')\n\n# Total fuel mass\ntotal_fuel_mass = base_fuel_mass + boil_off_loss + settling_loss + fuel_reserve\nprint(f'Total fuel mass: {total_fuel_mass:.0f} kg')\n\n# Check margin\nfuel_margin = (max_fuel_capacity - total_fuel_mass\n    max_possible_payload = min(max_payload, payload + available_fuel_capacity)\n    actual_payload = max_possible_payload\n    print(f'Maximum payload capacity: {actual_payload:.0f} kg')\nelse:\n    print('ERROR: Fuel requirements exceed spacecraft capacity!')\n\nprint(f'\\n=== FUEL BUDGET SUMMARY ===')\nprint(f'Base fuel mass: {base_fuel_mass:.0f} kg')\nprint(f'Boil-off loss: {boil_off_loss:.0f} kg ({boil_off_rate_per_day*100:.1f}%/day)')\nprint(f'Settling loss: {settling_loss:.0f} kg')  \nprint(f'Fuel reserve: {fuel_reserve:.0f} kg ({reserve_pct*100:.0f}%)')\nprint(f'Total fuel mass: {total_fuel_mass:.0f} kg')\nprint(f'Fuel margin: {fuel_margin:.1f}%')\nprint(f'Final payload: {actual_payload:.0f} kg')\nprint(f'Total wet mass: {total_wet_mass:.0f} kg')\nprint(f'Mass ratio: {actual_mass_ratio:.3f}')\n\"","description":"Calculate detailed fuel budget with all corrections"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":7,"cache_creation_input_tokens":541,"cache_read_input_tokens":40804,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":541,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff910e63-af4e-494a-9d0d-b2a308dcfdd0","uuid":"dfb90ae9-6aa2-45ea-9fd5-3a7f08fcf277"}
